# Documentation Rules

- Document commands that actually work in the current repository.
- Keep README setup instructions aligned with Docker Compose.
- Explain required environment variables without including secrets.
- Record significant decisions, alternatives, and trade-offs in DECISIONS.md.
- Include relevant failure modes and operational constraints.
- Update documentation when APIs, configuration, or behavior changes.
- Do not claim a service, feature, or test exists unless verified.
- Do not overwrite useful existing documentation with generic boilerplate.

# Frontend Rules

- Use Vue 3 Composition API and TypeScript with `strict` enabled.
- Keep components focused and reusable.
- Follow the existing component and state-management conventions.
- Avoid `any`; define explicit types for API responses and events.
- Keep API communication in a shared client or service layer.
- Handle loading, empty, error, and success states.
- Never store Claude API keys in frontend configuration.
- Treat all backend responses as untrusted data.
- Render user-controlled text safely; avoid v-html.
- Validate forms for usability, but rely on backend validation for security.
- Show final character counts after personalization where possible.
- Clean up SSE connections and event listeners on component unmount.

- Never overwrite unrelated user changes.

package campaign

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"regexp"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
)

// ID is a campaign's UUID in canonical lower-case form.
type ID string

var idPattern = regexp.MustCompile(`^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$`)

// ParseID validates s as a UUID and returns it in canonical form.
func ParseID(s string) (ID, error) {
	s = strings.ToLower(s)
	if !idPattern.MatchString(s) {
		return "", errors.New("campaign id must be a UUID")
	}
	return ID(s), nil
}

// Status is a campaign's lifecycle state. The backend is its only source
// of truth; clients display it.
type Status string

// Statuses, in lifecycle order.
const (
	// StatusDraft: created, no audience yet.
	StatusDraft Status = "draft"
	// StatusAudienceReady: a validated segment and its counts are stored.
	StatusAudienceReady Status = "audience_ready"
	// StatusDraftsReady: validated drafts are stored and can be edited.
	StatusDraftsReady Status = "drafts_ready"
	// StatusApproved: a human approved the exact content; immutable. Its
	// deliveries were created with the approval.
	StatusApproved Status = "approved"
	// StatusSending: a worker claimed the first delivery.
	StatusSending Status = "sending"
	// StatusCompleted: every delivery is sent or failed.
	StatusCompleted Status = "completed"
)

// transitions lists the legal status changes. drafts_ready → drafts_ready
// is a regeneration, which replaces the drafts. The delivery workers make
// the transitions after approved; approved → completed covers a campaign
// with nothing left to send.
var transitions = map[Status][]Status{
	StatusDraft:         {StatusAudienceReady},
	StatusAudienceReady: {StatusDraftsReady},
	StatusDraftsReady:   {StatusDraftsReady, StatusApproved},
	StatusApproved:      {StatusSending, StatusCompleted},
	StatusSending:       {StatusCompleted},
}

// CanTransition reports whether a campaign may move from one status to
// another.
func CanTransition(from, to Status) bool {
	return slices.Contains(transitions[from], to)
}

// Campaign is the aggregate the service loads, changes, and saves.
type Campaign struct {
	ID     ID
	Brief  string
	Status Status

	// Audience proposal, fixed at creation.
	Rule                segment.Rule
	Explanation         string
	UnsupportedCriteria []string
	Counts              segment.Counts

	// Reviewed content. Drafts is empty before drafts_ready.
	Channels []content.Channel
	Drafts   content.Drafts

	// Version increases with every content, channel, or status change and
	// is the optimistic lock for edits and approval.
	Version int
	// ContentHash binds approval to the content a reviewer saw; see
	// ContentHash.
	ContentHash string

	// A non-empty GenerationToken marks a draft generation in progress
	// until GenerationExpiresAt.
	GenerationToken     string
	GenerationExpiresAt time.Time

	ApprovedBy string
	ApprovedAt time.Time

	CreatedAt time.Time
	UpdatedAt time.Time
}

// HasDrafts reports whether the campaign has reviewable content.
func (c Campaign) HasDrafts() bool {
	return c.Status == StatusDraftsReady || c.Approved()
}

// Approved reports whether a human approved the campaign: it is approved,
// sending, or completed, and its content can no longer change.
func (c Campaign) Approved() bool {
	return c.Status == StatusApproved || c.Status == StatusSending || c.Status == StatusCompleted
}

// Generating reports whether a draft generation holds the campaign at now.
func (c Campaign) Generating(now time.Time) bool {
	return c.GenerationToken != "" && now.Before(c.GenerationExpiresAt)
}

// Validation is the content check shown to reviewers: the selected
// channels' fields against the channel rules, with each field's
// worst-case length.
type Validation struct {
	Valid     bool
	Errors    []content.FieldError
	WorstCase content.WorstCa

~~~~
package campaign

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
)

// Preview is a campaign's content rendered for real (fake) recipients,
// with every check approval would run.
type Preview struct {
	Campaign Campaign
	// Validation combines the static checks with the actual-audience
	// length and recipient checks.
	Validation Validation
	Channels   []ChannelPreview
}

// ChannelPreview is one selected channel's audience and samples.
type ChannelPreview struct {
	Channel    content.Channel
	Recipients int64
	Longest    map[content.Variable]int
	Samples    []SamplePreview
}

// SamplePreview is the channel's content rendered for one recipient.
type SamplePreview struct {
	Recipient Recipient
	Fields    []RenderedField
}

// RenderedField is a field after personalization, with its final length.
type RenderedField struct {
	Path      string
	Text      string
	Runes     int
	MaxRunes  int
	OverLimit bool
}

// Preview renders the campaign's content for sample recipients of each
// selected channel and runs approval's checks. Rendering happens only
// here, in the backend.
func (s *Service) Preview(ctx context.Context, id ID) (Preview, error) {
	c, err := s.Get(ctx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return Preview{}, err
	}
	if !c.HasDrafts() {
		return Preview{}, fail(CodeInvalidState, "the campaign has no drafts yet")
	}
	pred, err := segment.Compile(c.Rule, s.now().UTC())
	if err != nil {
		return Preview{}, fmt.Errorf("compile campaign segment: %w", err)
	}
	channels, audienceErrs, err := s.inspect(ctx, s.audience, c, pred, max(s.cfg.PreviewSamples, 1))
	if err != nil {
		return Preview{}, err
	}
	v := c.Validate()
	v.Errors = append(v.Errors, audienceErrs...)
	if !hasRecipients(channels) && len(c.Channels) > 0 {
		v.Errors = append(v.Errors, content.FieldError{Path: "channels", Message: messages[CodeNoRecipients]})
	}
	v.Valid = len(v.Errors) == 0
	return Preview{Campaign: c, Validation: v, Channels: channels}, nil
}

// inspect reads each selected channel's audience (users matching pred)
// and checks the content against its longest real values.
func (s *Service) inspect(ctx context.Context, audience AudienceInspector, c Campaign, pred segment.Predicate, samples int) ([]ChannelPreview, []content.FieldError, error) {
	var (
		out  []ChannelPreview
		errs []content.FieldError
	)
	for _, ch := range c.Channels {
		a, err := audience.InspectAudience(ctx, pred, ch, samples)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("inspect %s audience: %w", ch, err)
		}
		if a.Recipients > 0 {
			errs = append(errs, content.CheckAudienceLengths(c.Drafts, ch, a.Longest)...)
		}
		cp := ChannelPreview{Channel: ch, Recipients: a.Recipients, Longest: a.Longest}
		for _, r := range a.Samples {
			cp.Samples = append(cp.Samples, SamplePreview{Recipient: r, Fields: render(c.Drafts, ch, r)})
		}
		out = append(out, cp)
	}
	return out, errs, nil
}

func hasRecipients(channels []ChannelPreview) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(channels, func(c ChannelPreview) bool { return c.Recipients > 0 })
}

// render personalizes channel ch's fields for r. Fields that do not parse
// are omitted; validation reports them.
func render(d content.Drafts, ch content.Channel, r Recipient) []RenderedField {
	var out []RenderedField
	for _, f := range content.Fields(d, ch) {
		tmpl, err := content.ParseTemplate(f.Text)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		text := tmpl.Render(r.Values)
		n := utf8.RuneCountInString(text)
		out = append(out, RenderedField{Path: f.Path, Text: text, Runes: n, MaxRunes: f.MaxRunes, OverLimit: n > f.MaxRunes})
	}
	return out
}

func requireCode(t *testing.T, err error, want campaign.ErrorCode) {
	t.Helper()
	gerr, ok := errors.AsType[*campaign.Error](err)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("error = %v, want *campaign.Error with code %s", err, want)
	}
	if gerr.Code != want {
		t.Fatalf("code = %s (%v), want %s", gerr.Code, gerr, want)
	}
}

func (e fixtureEnv) create(t *testing.T) campaign.Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	c, err := e.svc.Create(t.Context(), exampleBrief)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Create() error = %v", err)
	}
	return c
}

func (e fixtureEnv) generate(t *testing.T, id campaign.ID) campaign.Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	g, err := e.svc.BeginGeneration(t.Context(), id)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("BeginGeneration() error = %v", err)
	}
	c, err := g.Run(t.Context(), nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Run() error = %v", err)
	}
	return c
}

// ready returns a campaign in drafts_ready with the mock's drafts.
func (e fixtureEnv) ready(t *testing.T) campaign.Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	return e.generate(t, e.create(t).ID)
}

func actions(events []campaign.Event) []string {
	out := make([]string, len(events))
	for i, ev := range events {
		out[i] = ev.Action
	}
	return out
}

func TestCreate(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	c := e.create(t)

	if c.Status != campaign.StatusAudienceReady || c.Version != 1 || c.ID == "" {
		t.Errorf("campaign = %+v", c)
	}
	if got := segment.Describe(c.Rule); !slices.Equal(got, []string{
		"City is İstanbul", "Added to cart in the last 30 days", "No purchase in the last 30 days",
	}) {
		t.Errorf("rule = %q", got)
	}
	if c.Counts != (segment.Counts{Total: 120, SMS: 80, Email: 95}) {
		t.Errorf("counts = %+v", c.Counts)
	}
	if !slices.Equal(c.Channels, []content.Channel{content.ChannelSMS, content.ChannelEmail}) {
		t.Errorf("channels = %v", c.Channels)
	}
	if c.ContentHash != campaign.ContentHash(c) {
		t.Error("content hash not set")
	}
	events := e.store.Events(c.ID)
	if !slices.Equal(actions(events), []string{campaign.ActionCreated, campaign.ActionAudienceProposed}) ||
		events[1].From != campaign.StatusDraft || events[1].To != campaign.StatusAudienceReady {
		t.Errorf("events = %+v", events)
	}
}

func TestCreate_FailuresStoreNothing(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name  string
		brief string
		step  mockllm.Step
		want  campaign.ErrorCode
	}{
		{"empty brief", "  ", mockllm.Step{}, campaign.CodeInvalidBrief},
		{"unsupported brief", "Send to users older than 30", mockllm.Step{}, campaign.CodeUnsupportedBrief},
		{"refusal", exampleBrief, mockllm.Step{Fault: mockllm.FaultRefusal}, campaign.CodeRefused},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			e := newEnv(t, tt.step)
			_, err := e.svc.Create(t.Context(), tt.brief)
			requireCode(t, err, tt.want)
			if list, _ := e.svc.List(t.Context()); len(list) != 0 {
				t.Errorf("stored %d campaigns", len(list))
			}
		})
	}
}

// edit applies mutate to an Edit built from c and returns the result.
func (e fixtureEnv) edit(t *testing.T, c campaign.Campaign, mutate func(*campaign.Edit)) campaign.Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	ed := editOf(c)
	mutate(&ed)
	out, err := e.svc.Edit(t.Context(), c.ID, ed)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Edit() error = %v", err)
	}
	return out
}

func editOf(c campaign.Campaign) campaign.Edit {
	names := make([]string, len(c.Channels))
	for i, ch := range c.Channels {
		names[i] = string(ch)
	}
	return campaign.Edit{Version: c.Version, Channels: names, Drafts: c.Drafts}
}

-- Campaigns and their audit log. The status CHECK mirrors the domain
-- state machine (internal/campaign); later milestones extend it with
-- sending states. Approval columns are set together with the approved
-- status, enforced below, so a half-approved row cannot exist.
CREATE TABLE campaigns (
    id                    uuid        PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuidv7(),
    brief                 text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(brief) BETWEEN 1 AND 1000),
    status                text        NOT NULL
        CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'audience_ready', 'drafts_ready', 'approved')),
    segment_rule          jsonb,
    segment_explanation   text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    unsupported_criteria  jsonb       NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]' CHECK (jsonb_typeof(unsupported_criteria) = 'array'),
    audience_total        bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (audience_total >= 0),
    audience_sms          bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (audience_sms >= 0),
    audience_email        bigint      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (audience_email >= 0),
    channels              text[]      NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}' CHECK (channels <@ ARRAY['sms', 'email']::text[]),
    sms_body              text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    email_subject         text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    email_body            text        NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    version               integer     NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (version >= 1),
    content_hash          text        NOT NULL CHECK (content_hash ~ '^[0-9a-f]{64}$'),
    -- Draft generation lease: at most one generation per campaign, held
    -- across API instances and expiring if its process dies.
    generation_token      text,
    generation_expires_at timestamptz,
    approved_by           text        CHECK (char_length(approved_by) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    approved_at           timestamptz,
    created_at            timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at            timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CHECK (status = 'draft' OR segment_rule IS NOT NULL),
    CHECK ((status = 'approved') = (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL)),
    CHECK ((generation_token IS NULL) = (generation_expires_at IS NULL))
);

CREATE INDEX campaigns_created_at_idx ON campaigns (created_at DESC);

-- Append-only audit of status transitions and edits: who, when, from → to.
-- It never stores message content or recipient data.
CREATE TABLE campaign_events (
    id          bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    campaign_id uuid        NOT NULL REFERENCES campaigns (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    action      text        NOT NULL
        CHECK (action IN ('created', 'audience_proposed', 'drafts_generated', 'edited', 'approved')),
    actor       text        NOT NULL CHECK (char_length(actor) BETWEEN 1 AND 100),
    from_status text,
    to_status   text        NOT NULL,
    version     integer     NOT NULL,
    created_at  timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX campaign_events_campaign_idx ON campaign_events (campaign_id, id);

-- Append-only: audit rows cannot be updated. Rows go away only with their
-- campaign (ON DELETE CASCADE); nothing in the application deletes either.
CREATE FUNCTION campaign_events_append_only() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'campaign_events is append-only';
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER campaign_events_no_update
    BEFORE UPDATE ON campaign_events
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION campaign_events_append_only();
-- Delivery: one row per (campaign, recipient, channel), a transactional
-- outbox that carries delivery jobs to Kafka, and a token bucket per
-- channel shared by every worker. Postgres is the source of truth for
-- delivery and rate-limit state; Kafka only transports work.

-- Sending states follow approval: approved → sending → completed. Every
-- approved-or-later campaign keeps its approver.
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP CONSTRAINT campaigns_status_check;
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD CONSTRAINT campaigns_status_c

package postgres

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/delivery"
)

// Every time comparison and timestamp in this file uses the database
// clock (clock_timestamp()), so leases, retry times, and rate-limit
// refills never depend on worker clocks.

// materializeDeliveries applies an approval's delivery plan inside the
// approval transaction: one delivery per user matching the predicate who
// granted consent for the channel, and one outbox message per delivery
// created. ON CONFLICT keeps existing rows, so re-applying creates nothing.
func materializeDeliveries(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, id campaign.ID, plan campaign.DeliveryPlan) error {
	n := len(plan.Predicate.Args)
	ph := func(i int) string { return fmt.Sprintf("$%d", n+i) }
	query := `WITH created AS (
		INSERT INTO deliveries (campaign_id, user_id, channel, max_attempts)
		SELECT ` + ph(1) + `, u.id, ` + ph(2) + `, ` + ph(3) + ` FROM users u
		WHERE (` + plan.Predicate.SQL + `) AND ` + fmt.Sprintf(consentFilter, ph(2)) + `
		ON CONFLICT (campaign_id, user_id, channel) DO NOTHING
		RETURNING id, channel
	)
	INSERT INTO outbox (topic, key, payload)
	SELECT ` + ph(4) + ` || channel, id::text, jsonb_build_object('delivery_id', id) FROM created`
	for _, ch := range plan.Channels {
		if ch != content.ChannelSMS && ch != content.ChannelEmail {
			return fmt.Errorf("materialize deliveries: unknown channel %q", ch)
		}
		args := append(append(make([]any, 0, n+4), plan.Predicate.Args...),
			string(id), string(ch), plan.MaxAttempts, delivery.TopicPrefix)
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, query, args...); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("materialize %s deliveries: %w", ch, err)
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// DeliveryStore implements delivery.Store, delivery.Maintenance, and
// delivery.Outbox.
type DeliveryStore struct {
	pool *pgxpool.Pool
}

var (
	_ delivery.Store       = (*DeliveryStore)(nil)
	_ delivery.Maintenance = (*DeliveryStore)(nil)
	_ delivery.Outbox      = (*DeliveryStore)(nil)
)

// NewDeliveryStore returns a store backed by pool.
func NewDeliveryStore(pool *pgxpool.Pool) *DeliveryStore {
	return &DeliveryStore{pool: pool}
}

// claimQuery claims a delivery and, for the campaign's first claim, moves
// the campaign from approved to sending with an audit event. Rows another
// transaction is updating are waited for, then re-checked, so exactly one
// concurrent claimer wins.
const claimQuery = `WITH claimed AS (
	UPDATE deliveries SET status = 'processing', attempts = attempts + 1,
		locked_until = clock_timestamp() + $2 * interval '1 microsecond', updated_at = clock_timestamp()
	WHERE id = $1 AND status IN ('queued', 'retrying') AND next_attempt_at <= clock_timestamp()
		AND attempts < max_attempts
	RETURNING id, campaign_id, user_id, channel, attempts, max_attempts
), started AS (
	UPDATE campaigns c SET status = 'sending', version = c.version + 1, updated_at = now()
	FROM claimed WHERE c.id = claimed.campaign_id AND c.status = 'approved'
	RETURNING c.id, c.version
), started_event AS (
	INSERT INTO campaign_events (campaign_id, action, actor, from_status, to_status, version)
	SELECT id, $3, $4, 'approved', 'sending', version FROM started
)
SELECT id::text, campaign_id::text, user_id, channel, attempts, max_attempts FROM claimed`

// Claim implements delivery.Store.
func (s *DeliveryStore) Claim(ctx context.Context, id delivery.ID, lease time.Duration) (delivery.Job, bool, error) {
	var (
		job     delivery.Job
		jid, ch string
	)
	err := s.pool.QueryRow(ctx, claimQuery, string(id), lease.Microseconds(),
		campaign.ActionSendingStarted, campaign.ActorSystem).
		Scan(&jid, &job.CampaignID, &job.UserID, &ch, &job.Attempt, &job.MaxAttempts)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return delivery.Job{}, false,

// variables. Every binary loads its configuration once at startup and fails

package config

import (
	"errors"

	"net/url"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

// Defaults applied when the corresponding variable is unset or empty.
const (
	DefaultAPIAddr          = ":8080"
	DefaultWorkerHealthAddr = ":8081"
	DefaultMockProviderAddr = ":9090"
	DefaultShutdownTimeout  = 10 * time.Second
	DefaultReadinessTimeout = 2 * time.Second
	DefaultRequestTimeout   = 30 * time.Second
	DefaultSeedUserCount    = 5000
	DefaultSeedRandomSeed   = 20261022
	MaxSeedUserCount        = 100000

	DefaultAnthropicModel    = "claude-sonnet-5-5"
	DefaultLLMSegmentTimeout = 20 * time.Second
	DefaultLLMDraftsTimeout  = 60 * time.Second
	DefaultLLMMaxAttempts    = 2
	DefaultLLMSDKMaxRetries  = 2
	// DefaultLLMMockStreamDelay paces the mock's streamed chunks so the UI
	// shows drafts arriving; about one second for the example drafts.
	DefaultLLMMockStreamDelay = 40 * time.Millisecond

	DefaultDeliveryMaxAttempts  = 5
	DefaultProviderURL          = "http://mock-provider:9090"
	DefaultProviderTimeout      = 5 * time.Second
	DefaultRetryBaseDelay       = time.Second
	DefaultRetryMaxDelay        = 60 * time.Second
	DefaultRetryBackoffFactor   = 2
	DefaultDeliveryLease        = 60 * time.Second
	DefaultWorkerConcurrency    = 16
	DefaultRateLimitPerSecond   = 90
	DefaultRateLimitBurst       = 5
	DefaultKafkaTopicPartitions = 6

	// Live progress: one shared poll per campaign and interval, whatever

	llmRouteSlack = 5 * time.Second
)

package httpapi

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/go-chi/chi/v5/middleware"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/platform/httpserver"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/strictjson"
)

// Request body limits. A maximal brief (1000 characters, each escaped as
// \uXXXX in the worst case) fits MaxBriefBodyBytes; a maximal edit
// (content at the service's hard caps, fully escaped) fits
// MaxEditBodyBytes.
const (
	MaxBriefBodyBytes   = 8 << 10
	MaxEditBodyBytes    = 32 << 10
	MaxApproveBodyBytes = 4 << 10
)

// StatusClientClosedRequest is recorded when the client disconnects before
// a response; nobody receives it, but access logs show what happened.
const StatusClientClosedRequest = 499

// CampaignService runs the campaign lifecycle. *campaign.Service
// implements it.
type CampaignService interface {
	Create(ctx context.Context, brief string) (campaign.Campaign, error)
	Get(ctx context.Context, id campaign.ID) (campaign.Campaign, error)
	List(ctx context.Context) ([]campaign.Campaign, error)
	Edit(ctx context.Context, id campaign.ID, e campaign.Edit) (campaign.Campaign, error)
	Preview(ctx context.Context, id campaign.ID) (campaign.Preview, error)
	Approve(ctx context.Context, id campaign.ID, a campaign.Approval) (campaign.Campaign, error)
	BeginGeneration(ctx context.Context, id campaign.ID) (*campaign.Generation, error)
}

// CampaignRoutes configures the campaign endpoints.
type CampaignRoutes struct {
	Service CampaignService
	Logger  *slog.Logger
	// RequestTimeout bounds reads, edits, previews, and approval.
	RequestTimeout time.Duration
	// CreateTimeout bounds creation, which calls the LLM.
	CreateTimeout time.Duration
	// Heartbeat is the interval of SSE keep-alive comments during draft
	// generation. The stream has no request timeout; the service bounds
	// the generation itself.
	Heartbeat time.Duration
	// Now reports the current time for derived fields; nil means time.Now.
	Now func() time.Time
}

// MountCampaigns registers the campaign routes on r, each with its own
// timeout.
func MountCampaigns(r chi.Router, cfg CampaignRoutes) {
	if cfg.Now == nil {
		cfg.Now = time.Now
	}
	h := campaignHandler{svc: cfg.Service, logger: cfg.Logger, heartbeat: cfg.Heartbeat, now: cfg.Now}
	timeout := middleware.Timeout(cfg.RequestTimeout)
	r.With(middleware.Timeout(cfg.CreateTimeout)).Post("/campaigns", h.create)
	r.With(timeout).Get("/campaigns", h.list)
	r.With(timeout).Get("/campaigns/{id}", h.get)
	r.With(timeout).Patch("/campaigns/{id}", h.edit)
	r.With(timeout).Get("/campaigns/{id}/preview", h.preview)
	r.With(timeout).Post("/campaigns/{id}/approve", h.approve)
	r.Post("/campaigns/{id}/drafts", h.generate)
}

type campaignHandler struct {
	svc       CampaignService
	logger    *slog.Logger
	heartbeat time.Duration
	now       func() time.Time
}

// EditRequest is the body of PATCH /campaigns/{id}. Every field is

// ApproveRequest is the body of POST /campaigns/{id}/approve: the version

type ApproveRequest struct {
	Version     *int    `json:"version"`
	ContentHash *string `json:"content_hash"`
	ApprovedBy  *string `json:"approved_by"`
}

	ID

<details><summary>Tool result</summary>

~~~~
// Package monitoring is the read model of campaign delivery progress: an
// aggregate snapshot of a campaign's deliveries, and a hub that polls it
// once per campaign for every live viewer.
//
// Snapshots are full state, never increments, so a client that reconnects
// or receives the same snapshot twice can only display correct numbers.
//
// The package has no HTTP or SQL dependencies.
package monitoring

import (
	"cmp"
	"context"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/delivery"
)

// StatusCounts counts deliveries by status.
type StatusCounts struct {
	Queued     int64
	Processing int64
	Sent       int64
	Failed     int64
	Retrying   int64
}

// Total is the number of deliveries counted.
func (c StatusCounts) Total() int64 {
	return c.Queued + c.Processing + c.Sent + c.Failed + c.Retrying
}

// Done is the number of terminal (sent or failed) deliveries.
func (c StatusCounts) Done() int64 { return c.Sent + c.Failed }

// PercentDone is the share of terminal deliveries, 0–100; 0 without
// deliveries.
func (c StatusCounts) PercentDone() float64 {
	if c.Total() == 0 {
		return 0
	}
	return float64(c.Done()) * 100 / float64(c.Total())
}

// Add counts n deliveries in status s. Unknown statuses are ignored.
func (c *StatusCounts) Add(s delivery.Status, n int64) {
	switch s {
	case delivery.StatusQueued:
		c.Queued += n
	case delivery.StatusProcessing:
		c.Processing += n
	case delivery.StatusSent:
		c.Sent += n
	case delivery.StatusFailed:
		c.Failed += n
	case delivery.StatusRetrying:
		c.Retrying += n
	}
}

func (c StatusCounts) plus(o StatusCounts) StatusCounts {
	return StatusCounts{
		Queued:     c.Queued + o.Queued,
		Processing: c.Processing + o.Processing,
		Sent:       c.Sent + o.Sent,
		Failed:     c.Failed + o.Failed,
		Retrying:   c.Retrying + o.Retrying,
	}
}

// ChannelProgress is one channel's deliveries.
type ChannelProgress struct {
	Channel content.Channel
	Counts  StatusCounts
	// RecentSent counts deliveries sent within the snapshot's window.
	RecentSent int64
}

// FailureCount is the number of failed deliveries with one error code.
type FailureCount struct {
	Code  string
	Count int64
}

// Snapshot is a campaign's delivery progress at one moment.
type Snapshot struct {
	CampaignID     campaign.ID
	CampaignStatus campaign.Status
	// Channels lists the campaign's channels in a fixed order (sms, email).
	Channels []ChannelProgress
	// Failures lists failed deliveries by error code, most frequent first.
	Failures []FailureCount
	// Window is the period RecentSent covers.
	Window      time.Duration
	GeneratedAt time.Time
}

// Totals sums the channels' counts.
func (s Snapshot) Totals() StatusCounts {
	var t StatusCounts
	for _, ch := range s.Channels {
		t = t.plus(ch.Counts)
	}
	return t
}

// RecentSent sums the channels' recent sends.
func (s Snapshot) RecentSent() int64 {
	var n int64
	for _, ch := range s.Channels {
		n += ch.RecentSent
	}
	return n
}

// Throughput is the send rate over the window, in messages per second.
func (s Snapshot) Throughput() float64 {
	if s.Window <= 0 {
		return 0
	}
	return float64(s.RecentSent()) / s.Window.Seconds()
}

// PercentDone is the share of terminal deliveries, 0–100. A completed
// campaign without deliveries is 100% done.
func (s Snapshot) PercentDone() float64 {
	t := s.Totals()
	if t.Total() == 0 && s.Completed() {
		return 100
	}
	return t.PercentDone()
}

// Completed reports whether the campaign finished sending: no snapshot
// after this one can differ.
func (s Snapshot) Completed() bool { return s.CampaignStatus == campaign.StatusCompleted }

// SameState reports whether s and o describe the same progress, ignoring
// when they were generated.
func (s Snapshot) SameState(o Snapshot) bool {
	return s.CampaignID == o.CampaignID && s.CampaignStatus == o.CampaignStatus &&
… [truncated 7336 chars]

	Count int64  `json:"count"`

// Throughput is the recent send rate across channels.

	WindowSeconds float64 `json:"window_seconds"`
	Sent          int64   `json:"sent"`
	PerSecond     float64 `json:"per_second"`
}

// Delivery outcome label values.
const (
	OutcomeSent     = "sent"
	OutcomeFailed   = "failed"
	OutcomeRetrying = "retrying"
	// OutcomeReleased: shutdown interrupted the attempt before it counted.
	OutcomeReleased = "released"
)

var channels = []content.Channel{content.ChannelSMS, content.ChannelEmail}

// NewDelivery registers the delivery metrics on reg.
func NewDelivery(reg prometheus.Registerer) *Delivery {
	m := &Delivery{
		deliveries: prometheus.NewCounterVec(prometheus.CounterOpts{
			Name: "deliveries_total",
			Help: "Recorded delivery attempts by channel and outcome (sent, failed, retrying, released).",
		}, []string{"channel", "outcome"}),
		providerDuration: prometheus.NewHistogramVec(prometheus.HistogramOpts{
			Name:    "provider_request_duration_seconds",
			Help:    "Messaging provider request duration by channel.",
			Buckets: []float64{.005, .01, .025, .05, .1, .25, .5, 1, 2.5, 5, 10},
		}, []string{"channel"}),
		providerCodes: prometheus.NewCounterVec(prometheus.CounterOpts{
			Name: "provider_responses_total",
			Help: "Messaging provider responses by channel and code class (2xx, 3xx, 4xx, 429, 5xx, error).",
		}, []string{"channel", "code_class"}),
		retries: prometheus.NewCounterVec(prometheus.CounterOpts{
			Name: "retries_scheduled_total",
			Help: "Delivery retries scheduled by channel and reason (error code).",
		}, []string{"channel", "reason"}),
		limiterWait: prometheus.NewHistogramVec(prometheus.HistogramOpts{
			Name:    "rate_limiter_wait_seconds",
			Help:    "Time a delivery attempt waited for the shared per-channel rate limit.",
			Buckets: []float64{.001, .005, .01, .025, .05, .1, .25, .5, 1, 2.5, 5, 10, 30},
		}, []string{"channel"}),
		outboxPending: prometheus.NewGauge(prometheus.GaugeOpts{
			Name: "outbox_pending",
			Help: "Unpublished outbox messages, as last counted by this worker (a global value: every worker reports it).",
		}),
		leasesRecovered: prometheus.NewCounter(prometheus.CounterOpts{
			Name: "leases_recovered_total",
			Help: "Deliveries returned to retrying or failed after their worker's lease expired.",
		}),
	}
	reg.MustRegister(m.deliveries, m.providerDuration, m.providerCodes, m.retries, m.limiterWait,
		m.outboxPending, m.leasesRecovered)
	// Start the main series at zero, so rates are defined from the first
	// scrape.
	for _, ch := range channels {
		for _, o := range []string{OutcomeSent, OutcomeFailed, OutcomeRetrying, OutcomeReleased} {
			m.deliveries.WithLabelValues(string(ch), o)
		}
	}
	return m
}

// AttemptRecorded implements delivery.Metrics.
func (m *Delivery) AttemptRecorded(c content.Channel, o delivery.Outcome) {
	switch {
	case o.Release:
		m.deliveries.WithLabelValues(string(c), OutcomeReleased).Inc()
	case o.Status == delivery.StatusSent:
		m.deliveries.WithLabelValues(string(c), OutcomeSent).Inc()
	case o.Status == delivery.StatusFailed:
		m.deliveries.WithLabelValues(string(c), OutcomeFailed).Inc()
	case o.Status == delivery.StatusRetrying:
		m.deliveries.WithLabelValues(string(c), OutcomeRetrying).Inc()
		m.retries.WithLabelValues(string(c), o.ErrorCode).Inc()
	}
}

// RateLimitWaited implements delivery.Metrics.
func (m *Delivery) RateLimitWaited(c content.Channel, d time.Duration) {
	m.limiterWait.WithLabelValues(string(c)).Observe(d.Seconds())
}

// LeasesRecovered implements delivery.Metrics.
func (m *Delivery) LeasesRecovered(n int) { m.leasesRecovered.Add(float64(n)) }

// OutboxPending implements delivery.Metrics.
func (m *Delivery) OutboxPending(n int) { m.outboxPending.Set(float64(n)) }

func newToken() (string, error) {
	b := make([]byte, 16)
	if _, err := rand.Read(b); err != nil {
		return "", fmt.Errorf("generation token: %w", err)
	}
	return hex.EncodeToString(b), nil
}

// Run generates drafts, streaming field text to onDelta (display only),
// and stores them once they pass validation, moving the campaign to
// drafts_ready. On any failure, including cancellation by the caller, the
// campaign keeps its previous content and status and the lease is
// released.
func (g *Generation) Run(ctx context.Context, onDelta func(FieldDelta)) (Campaign, error) {
	genCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, g.s.cfg.GenerationTimeout)
	defer cancel()

	var x *fieldExtractor
	attempt := 0
	res, err := g.s.gen.GenerateDrafts(genCtx, g.brief, g.summary, func(d Delta) {
		if onDelta == nil {
			return
		}
		if d.Attempt != attempt {
			attempt, x = d.Attempt, newFieldExtractor()
		}
		for _, fd := range x.Feed(d.Text) {
			fd.Attempt = attempt
			onDelta(fd)
		}
	})

	// Saving and releasing must happen even if the client has gone.
	cleanupCtx, cleanupCancel := context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), cleanupTimeout)
	defer cleanupCancel()
	if err != nil {
		g.release(cleanupCtx)
		return Campaign{}, err
	}
	c, err := g.s.update(cleanupCtx, g.id, func(_ context.Context, _ AudienceInspector, c Campaign) (*Write, error) {
		// An expired lease may have let an edit or another generation in;
		// saving now would silently overwrite it.
		if c.GenerationToken != g.token || !c.Generating(g.s.now()) {
			return nil, fail(CodeInvalidState, "the generation lease expired before the drafts were ready")
		}
		if !CanTransition(c.Status, StatusDraftsReady) {
			return nil, fail(CodeInvalidState, "")
		}
		from := c.Status
		c.Status, c.Drafts = StatusDraftsReady, res.Drafts
		c.GenerationToken, c.GenerationExpiresAt = "", time.Time{}
		c.Version++
		c.ContentHash = ContentHash(c)
		return &Write{Campaign: c, Events: []Event{{
			Action: ActionDraftsGenerated, Actor: ActorSystem, From: from, To: c.Status, Version: c.Version,
		}}}, nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		g.release(cleanupCtx)
		return Campaign{}, err
	}
	return c, nil
}

// release clears the lease if this generation still holds it.
func (g *Generation) release(ctx context.Context) {
	_, err := g.s.update(ctx, g.id, func(_ context.Context, _ AudienceInspector, c Campaign) (*Write, error) {
		if c.GenerationToken != g.token {
			return nil, nil
		}
		c.GenerationToken, c.GenerationExpiresAt = "", time.Time{}
		return &Write{Campaign: c}, nil
	})
	if err != nil {
		// The lease expires on its own; log so a stuck campaign is explainable.
		g.s.logger.WarnContext(ctx, "release generation lease failed", "campaign_id", string(g.id), "error", err)
	}
}

package campaign

import (
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/hex"
	"testing"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/content"
	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/segment"
)

func TestCanTransition(t *testing.T) {
	legal := map[[2]Status]bool{
		{StatusDraft, StatusAudienceReady}:       true,
		{StatusAudienceReady, StatusDraftsReady}: true,
		{StatusDraftsReady, StatusDraftsReady}:   true, // regenerate drafts
		{StatusDraftsReady, StatusApproved}:      true,
		{StatusApproved, StatusSending}:          true, // first delivery claimed
		{StatusApproved, StatusCompleted}:        true, // no delivery was left to send
		{StatusSending, StatusCompleted}:         true,
	}
	all := []Status{StatusDraft, StatusAudienceReady, StatusDraftsReady, StatusApproved, StatusSending, StatusCompleted,
		Status("cancelled"), Status("")}
	for _, from := range all {
		for _, to := range all {
			want := legal[[2]Status{from, to}]
			if got := CanTransition(from, to); got != want {
				t.Errorf("CanTransition(%q, %q) = %v, want %v", from, to, got, want)
			}
		}
	}
}

func TestParseID(t *testing.T) {
	valid := []string{"0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e", "0196F0A2-7C1E-7B3A-9D2E-3F4A5B6C7D8E"}
	for _, s := range valid {
		if id, err := ParseID(s); err != nil || string(id) != "0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e" {
			t.Errorf("ParseID(%q) = %q, %v", s, id, err)
		}
	}
	invalid := []string{"", "1", "0196f0a27c1e7b3a9d2e3f4a5b6c7d8e", "0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8", "zzzzzzzz-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e",
		"0196f0a2-7c1e-7b3a-9d2e-3f4a5b6c7d8e'--"}
	for _, s := range invalid {
		if _, err := ParseID(s); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("ParseID(%q) succeeded", s)
		}
	}
}

func hashFixture(t *testing.T) Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	rule, err := segment.Parse([]byte(`{"version":1,"conditions":[{"field":"city","op":"eq","value":"İstanbul"}]}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return Campaign{
		Rule:     rule,
		Channels: []content.Channel{content.ChannelSMS, content.ChannelEmail},
		Drafts: content.Drafts{
			SMS:   content.SMS{Body: "Merhaba {{first_name}}"},
			Email: content.Email{Subject: "Konu", Body: "Gövde"},
		},
		Version: 3,
		Brief:   "brief",
	}
}

package campaign_test

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"
)

// scheduleOf approves c for at.
func scheduleOf(c campaign.Campaign, at time.Time) campaign.Approval {
	a := approvalOf(c)
	a.ScheduledAt = at
	return a
}

func (e fixtureEnv) schedule(t *testing.T, c campaign.Campaign, in time.Duration) campaign.Campaign {
	t.Helper()
	out, err := e.svc.Approve(t.Context(), c.ID, scheduleOf(c, e.clock.Now().Add(in)))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Approve(scheduled) error = %v", err)
	}
	return out
}

func TestApprove_Scheduled(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	c := e.ready(t)
	at := e.clock.Now().Add(2 * time.Hour)
	got, err := e.svc.Approve(t.Context(), c.ID, scheduleOf(c, at))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Approve() error = %v", err)
	}
	if got.Status != campaign.StatusScheduled || !got.ScheduledAt.Equal(at) || got.ApprovedBy != "Deniz Operator" ||
		!got.ApprovedAt.Equal(e.clock.Now()) || got.Version != c.Version+1 || got.ContentHash != c.ContentHash {
		t.Errorf("scheduled campaign = %+v", got)
	}
	if plans := e.store.Plans(c.ID); len(plans) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("scheduled approval planned deliveries: %+v", plans)
	}
	events := e.store.Events(c.ID)
	last := events[len(events)-1]
	if last.Action != campaign.ActionScheduled || last.Actor != "Deniz Operator" ||
		last.From != campaign.StatusDraftsReady || last.To != campaign.StatusScheduled || last.Version != got.Version {
		t.Errorf("schedule event = %+v", last)
	}
}
EOF
go test ./internal/campaign/ -run TestApprove_Scheduled 2>&1 | tail -5

// (approved or later, or approved for a scheduled time), so its content
// can no longer change. A cancel removes the approval of a campaign that
// was not sent.
func (c Campaign) HoldsApproval() bool {
	return c.Approved() || c.Status == StatusScheduled || c.Status == StatusReleaseFailed || c.Status == StatusExpired
}

**🔧 Bash** — Refactor Approve with shared checks and schedule support; run tests

~~~~
python3 - <<'EOF'
p='internal/campaign/approve.go'
s=open(p).read()
start=s.index("// MaxApproverRunes")
end=s.index("// revalidateRule runs")
new='''// MaxApproverRunes caps the free-text approver (or actor) name.
const MaxApproverRunes = 100

// Approval is a human's approval request. Version and ContentHash are the
// values the reviewer saw. A non-zero ScheduledAt approves the campaign
// for release at that time instead of sending now.
type Approval struct {
	Version     int
	ContentHash string
	ApprovedBy  string
	ScheduledAt time.Time
}

// Approve records explicit human approval of exactly the reviewed content.
// Inside one transaction it re-checks the version and content hash,
// re-validates the segment rule and content, checks lengths against the
// longest real values of each selected channel's consented recipients,
// and requires at least one recipient.
//
// Without a scheduled time, the same transaction creates one delivery per
// consented recipient and channel, compiled from the same predicate the
// checks used. With one, the campaign becomes scheduled and nothing is
// created: Release repeats the checks and creates the deliveries at that
// time.
//
// Approving an approved (or later) or scheduled campaign with the same
// content hash returns it unchanged, schedule included, and creates
// nothing.
func (s *Service) Approve(ctx context.Context, id ID, a Approval) (Campaign, error) {
	approver, err := checkActor(a.ApprovedBy, "approved_by")
	if err != nil {
		return Campaign{}, err
	}
	return s.update(ctx, id, func(ctx context.Context, audience AudienceInspector, c Campaign) (*Write, error) {
		now := s.now()
		if c.Approved() || c.Status == StatusScheduled {
			if a.ContentHash == c.ContentHash {
				return nil, nil
			}
			return nil, fail(CodeContentChanged, "the campaign is already approved with different content")
		}
		scheduled := !a.ScheduledAt.IsZero()
		to := StatusApproved
		if scheduled {
			to = StatusScheduled
			if err := s.checkScheduledAt(a.ScheduledAt, now); err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
		}
		switch {
		case !CanTransition(c.Status, to) || c.Status != StatusDraftsReady:
			return nil, fail(CodeInvalidState, "only campaigns with drafts can be approved")
		case c.Generating(now):
			return nil, fail(CodeGenerationInProgress, "")
		case a.Version != c.Version:
			return nil, fail(CodeVersionConflict, "")
		case a.ContentHash != c.ContentHash || ContentHash(c) != c.ContentHash:
			return nil, fail(CodeContentChanged, "")
		}
		pred, err := s.checkApproval(ctx, audience, c, now)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		from := c.Status
		c.Status, c.ApprovedBy, c.ApprovedAt = to, approver, now
		c.Version++
		action := ActionApproved
		var plan *DeliveryPlan
		if scheduled {
			action, c.ScheduledAt = ActionScheduled, a.ScheduledAt
		} else {
			plan = s.deliveryPlan(c, pred)
		}
		return &Write{
			Campaign:   c,
			Events:     []Event{{Action: action, Actor: approver, From: from, To: c.Status, Version: c.Version}},
			Deliveries: plan,
		}, nil
	})
}

// checkApproval runs every content and audience check of an approval or
// release: the segment rule and content are re-validated, the rule is
// compiled with ref as reference time, lengths are checked against the
// longest real values of each selected channel's consented recipients,
// and at least one recipient is required. It returns the compiled
// predicate, so deliveries target exactly the audience that was checked.
// Check failures are *Error; anything else is an infrastructure error.
func (s *Service) checkApproval(ctx context.Context, audience AudienceInspector, c Campaign, ref time.Time) (segment.Predicate, error) {
	if err := revalidateRule(c.Rule); err != nil {
		return segment.Predicate{}, err
	}
	if v := c.Validate(); !v.Valid {
		return segment.Predicate{}, fail(CodeInvalidContent, "", v.Errors...)
	}

func TestApprove_ScheduledTimeRules(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name string
		in   time.Duration
		ok   bool
	}{
		{"in the past", -time.Second, false},
		{"now", 0, false},
		{"below the minimum lead", 59 * time.Second, false},
		{"exactly the minimum lead", time.Minute, true},
		{"exactly the maximum horizon", 30 * 24 * time.Hour, true},
		{"beyond the maximum horizon", 30*24*time.Hour + time.Second, false},
	}
	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			e := newEnv(t)
			c := e.ready(t)
			got, err := e.svc.Approve(t.Context(), c.ID, scheduleOf(c, e.clock.Now().Add(tt.in)))
			if tt.ok {
				if err != nil || got.Status != campaign.StatusScheduled {
					t.Fatalf("Approve() = %s, %v; want scheduled", got.Status, err)
				}
				return
			}
			requireCode(t, err, campaign.CodeInvalidRequest)
			requireDetail(t, err, "scheduled_at")
			if got, _ := e.svc.Get(t.Context(), c.ID); got.Status != campaign.StatusDraftsReady {
				t.Errorf("rejected schedule changed status to %s", got.Status)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestParseScheduledAt(t *testing.T) {
	want := time.Date(2026, 10, 30, 10, 0, 0, 0, time.FixedZone("", 3*3600))
	for _, s := range []string{"2026-10-30T10:00:00+03:00", "2026-10-30T07:00:00Z", "2026-10-30T07:00:00.000Z"} {
		got, err := campaign.ParseScheduledAt(s)
		if err != nil || !got.Equal(want) {
			t.Errorf("ParseScheduledAt(%q) = %v, %v; want %v", s, got, err, want)
		}
	}
	for _, s := range []string{"", "2026-10-30T10:00:00", "2026-10-30 10:00:00+03:00", "2026-10-30", "tomorrow",
		"2026-13-30T10:00:00Z", "2026-10-30T10:00:00+3", "10000-01-01T00:00:00Z"} {
		_, err := campaign.ParseScheduledAt(s)
		requireCode(t, err, campaign.CodeInvalidRequest)
		requireDetail(t, err, "scheduled_at")
	}
}

func requireDetail(t *testing.T, err error, path string) {
	t.Helper()
	gerr, _ := errors.AsType[*campaign.Error](err)
	for _, d := range gerr.Details {
		if d.Path == path {
			return
		}
	}
	t.Errorf("details = %+v, want one for %s", gerr.Details, path)
}
EOF

**🔧 Bash** — Implement ParseScheduledAt and run tests

~~~~
cat >> internal/campaign/schedule.go <<'EOF'

// ParseScheduledAt parses a scheduled time: RFC 3339 with an explicit
// offset ("Z" or ±hh:mm), so a client never has its time read in a zone it
// did not choose.
func ParseScheduledAt(s string) (time.Time, error) {
	t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, s)
	if err != nil {
		return time.Time{}, fail(CodeInvalidRequest, "",
			content.FieldError{Path: "scheduled_at", Message: "must be an RFC 3339 time with an offset, such as 2026-10-30T10:00:00+03:00"})
	}
	return t, nil
}
EOF

func TestApprove_ScheduledIsIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	c := e.ready(t)
	first := e.schedule(t, c, 2*time.Hour)

	// Same hash with another time or no time: unchanged, schedule included.
	for _, at := range []time.Time{e.clock.Now().Add(5 * time.Hour), {}} {
		again, err := e.svc.Approve(t.Context(), c.ID, scheduleOf(c, at))
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("re-approve error = %v", err)
		}
		if again.Status != campaign.StatusScheduled || again.Version != first.Version || !again.ScheduledAt.Equal(first.ScheduledAt) {
			t.Errorf("re-approval changed the campaign: %+v", again)
		}
	}
	if n := len(e.store.Plans(c.ID)); n != 0 {
		t.Errorf("re-approval planned %d deliveries", n)
	}
	other := approvalOf(c)
	other.ContentHash = c.ContentHash[:63] + "x"
	_, err := e.svc.Approve(t.Context(), c.ID, other)
	requireCode(t, err, campaign.CodeContentChanged)
}

func TestScheduledStatusesAreImmutable(t *testing.T) {
	for _, status := range []campaign.Status{campaign.StatusScheduled, campaign.StatusReleaseFailed, campaign.StatusExpired} {
		t.Run(string(status), func(t *testing.T) {
			e := newEnv(t)
			c := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), time.Hour)
			c.Status = status
			e.store.Put(c)
			if !c.HasDrafts() || !c.HoldsApproval() || c.Approved() {
				t.Errorf("HasDrafts=%v HoldsApproval=%v Approved=%v", c.HasDrafts(), c.HoldsApproval(), c.Approved())
			}
			_, err := e.svc.Edit(t.Context(), c.ID, campaign.Edit{Version: c.Version, Drafts: c.Drafts, Channels: []string{"sms"}})
			requireCode(t, err, campaign.CodeInvalidState)
			_, err = e.svc.BeginGeneration(t.Context(), c.ID)
			requireCode(t, err, campaign.CodeInvalidState)
		})
	}
}

func TestApprove_ReleaseFailedOrExpiredNeedsCancel(t *testing.T) {
	for _, status := range []campaign.Status{campaign.StatusReleaseFailed, campaign.StatusExpired} {
		t.Run(string(status), func(t *testing.T) {

			c := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), time.Hour)

			_, err := e.svc.Approve(t.Context(), c.ID, approvalOf(c))
			requireCode(t, err, campaign.CodeInvalidState)

	}
}

python3 - <<'EOF'
p='internal/campaign/schedule.go'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('import (\n\t"fmt"\n\t"time"\n','import (\n\t"context"\n\t"fmt"\n\t"time"\n')
s+='''
// Reschedule is a human's request to move a scheduled campaign. Version
// and ContentHash are the values the actor saw.
type Reschedule struct {
	Version     int
	ContentHash string
	ScheduledAt time.Time
	Actor       string
}

// Reschedule moves a scheduled campaign to another time, keeping its
// approval of the unchanged content. Under the campaign lock it serializes
// with Release: once a campaign was released, it fails with
// CodeInvalidState.
func (s *Service) Reschedule(ctx context.Context, id ID, r Reschedule) (Campaign, error) {
	actor, err := checkActor(r.Actor, "actor")
	if err != nil {
		return Campaign{}, err
	}
	if r.ScheduledAt.IsZero() {
		return Campaign{}, fail(CodeInvalidRequest, "", content.FieldError{Path: "scheduled_at", Message: "is required"})
	}
	return s.update(ctx, id, func(_ context.Context, _ AudienceInspector, c Campaign) (*Write, error) {
		if err := checkHeld(c, StatusScheduled, r.Version, r.ContentHash, "only scheduled campaigns can be rescheduled"); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if err := s.checkScheduledAt(r.ScheduledAt, s.now()); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		c.ScheduledAt = r.ScheduledAt
		c.Version++
		return &Write{Campaign: c, Events: []Event{{
			Action: ActionRescheduled, Actor: actor, From: StatusScheduled, To: StatusScheduled, Version: c.Version,
		}}}, nil
	})
}

// checkHeld checks a human action on an approved-for-later campaign: the
// transition to to is legal, and version and hash are the ones the actor
// saw.
func checkHeld(c Campaign, to Status, version int, hash, stateMsg string) error {
	switch {
	case !c.HoldsApproval() || c.Approved() || !CanTransition(c.Status, to):
		return fail(CodeInvalidState, stateMsg)
	case version != c.Version:
		return fail(CodeVersionConflict, "")
	case hash != c.ContentHash:
		return fail(CodeContentChanged, "")
	}
	return nil
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

// Cancel is a human's request to stop a campaign approved for later.
// Version and ContentHash are the values the actor saw.
type Cancel struct {
	Version     int
	ContentHash string
	Actor       string
}

// Cancel returns a scheduled, release_failed, or expired campaign to
// drafts_ready: the approval and the schedule are cleared and the version
// moves on, so sending it needs a fresh review and approval. Under the
// campaign lock it serializes with Release: once a campaign was released,
// it fails with CodeInvalidState.
func (s *Service) Cancel(ctx context.Context, id ID, r Cancel) (Campaign, error) {
	actor, err := checkActor(r.Actor, "actor")
	if err != nil {
		return Campaign{}, err
	}
	return s.update(ctx, id, func(_ context.Context, _ AudienceInspector, c Campaign) (*Write, error) {
		if err := checkHeld(c, StatusDraftsReady, r.Version, r.ContentHash,
			"only scheduled, release_failed, or expired campaigns can be canceled"); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		from := c.Status
		c.Status = StatusDraftsReady
		c.ApprovedBy, c.ApprovedAt, c.ScheduledAt, c.ReleaseFailure = "", time.Time{}, time.Time{}, nil
		c.Version++
		return &Write{Campaign: c, Events: []Event{{
			Action: ActionScheduleCanceled, Actor: actor, From: from, To: c.Status, Version: c.Version,
		}}}, nil
	})
}
'''
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF
go test ./internal/campaign/ -run 'Cancel' 2>&1 | tail -15

package campaign_test

import (
	"errors"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"

func lastEvent(e fixtureEnv, id campaign.ID) campaign.Event {
	events := e.store.Events(id)
	return events[len(events)-1]
}

func TestRelease_BeforeDueIsNoOp(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	c := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), time.Hour)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour - time.Second)
	res, err := e.svc.Release(t.Context(), c.ID)
	if err != nil || res.Outcome != campaign.ReleaseNone {
		t.Fatalf("Release() = %+v, %v; want no-op", res, err)
	}
	if got, _ := e.svc.Get(t.Context(), c.ID); got.Status != campaign.StatusScheduled || got.Version != c.Version {
		t.Errorf("campaign changed: %+v", got)
	}
}

func TestRelease_AtDue(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	c := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), time.Hour)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour + 30*time.Second)

	res, err := e.svc.Release(t.Context(), c.ID)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Release() error = %v", err)
	}
	if res.Outcome != campaign.ReleaseReleased || res.Lateness != 30*time.Second {
		t.Errorf("result = %+v, want released 30s late", res)
	}
	got, _ := e.svc.Get(t.Context(), c.ID)
	if got.Status != campaign.StatusApproved || got.Version != c.Version+1 || !got.ScheduledAt.Equal(c.ScheduledAt) ||
		got.ApprovedBy != c.ApprovedBy || !got.ApprovedAt.Equal(c.ApprovedAt) || got.ContentHash != c.ContentHash {
		t.Errorf("released = %+v", got)
	}
	ev := lastEvent(e, c.ID)
	if ev.Action != campaign.ActionReleased || ev.Actor != campaign.ActorSystem ||
		ev.From != campaign.StatusScheduled || ev.To != campaign.StatusApproved || ev.Version != got.Version {
		t.Errorf("release event = %+v", ev)
	}
	plans := e.store.Plans(c.ID)
	if len(plans) != 1 || plans[0].MaxAttempts != 4 || len(plans[0].Channels) != len(c.Channels) {
		t.Fatalf("plans = %+v, want one with the release", plans)
	}
	// Day windows are measured from the scheduled time, not from approval
	// or from the moment the worker got to it.
	var fromScheduled, fromClock bool
	for _, a := range plans[0].Predicate.Args {
		if ts, ok := a.(time.Time); ok {
			fromScheduled = fromScheduled || ts.Equal(c.ScheduledAt)
			fromClock = fromClock || ts.Equal(e.clock.Now()) || ts.Equal(c.ApprovedAt)
		}
	}
	if !fromScheduled || fromClock {
		t.Errorf("predicate args %v: want the scheduled time %v as reference", plans[0].Predicate.Args, c.ScheduledAt)
	}

	// Releasing again is a no-op.
	res, err = e.svc.Release(t.Context(), c.ID)
	if err != nil || res.Outcome != campaign.ReleaseNone || len(e.store.Plans(c.ID)) != 1 {
		t.Errorf("second Release() = %+v, %v, plans %d", res, err, len(e.store.Plans(c.ID)))
	}
}

type recordedRelease struct {

func TestReleaseDue(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	rec := &releaseRecorder{}
	e.svc = e.withReleaseMetrics(rec)
	e.store.Now = e.clock.Now

	soon := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), time.Hour)
	sooner := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), 30*time.Minute)
	later := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), 3*time.Hour)
	ready := e.ready(t)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour + time.Minute)

}

func (s *Store) DueScheduled(_ context.Context, limit int) ([]campaign.ID, error) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	now := time.Now()
	if s.Now != nil {
		now = s.Now()
	}
	var due []campaign.Campaign
	for _, id := range s.order {
		c := s.campaigns[id]
		if c.Status == campaign.StatusScheduled && !c.ScheduledAt.After(now) {
			due = append(due, c)
		}
	}
	slices.SortStableFunc(due, func(a, b campaign.Campaign) int { return a.ScheduledAt.Compare(b.ScheduledAt) })
	out := make([]campaign.ID, 0, min(limit, len(due)))
	for _, c := range due[:min(limit, len(due))] {
		out = append(out, c.ID)
	}
	return out, nil
}

func (s *Service) ReleaseDue(ctx context.Context, limit int) (int, error) {
	ids, err := s.store.DueScheduled(ctx, limit)
	if err != nil {
		return 0, fmt.Errorf("find due campaigns: %w", err)
	}
	n := 0
	for _, id := range ids {

		if err != nil {

				return n, ctx.Err()
			}
			s.logger.WarnContext(ctx, "release scheduled campaign failed; retrying later", "campaign_id", string(id), "error", err)

			n++
			s.logger.InfoContext(ctx, "scheduled campaign released", "campaign_id", string(id),
				"outcome", string(res.Outcome), "lateness", res.Lateness.String())
		}
	}
	return n, nil
}

Domain is green. Adding a concurrency test for release vs. cancel at the domain seam, then moving to Postgres.

**🔧 Bash** — Add release-vs-cancel race test and run

~~~~
cat >> internal/campaign/release_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestRelease_ConcurrentWithCancel(t *testing.T) {
	e := newEnv(t)
	c := e.schedule(t, e.ready(t), time.Hour)
	e.clock.Advance(time.Hour)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	var cancelErr error
	for range 5 {
		wg.Go(func() {
			if _, err := e.svc.Release(t.Context(), c.ID); err != nil {
				t.Errorf("Release() error = %v", err)
			}
		})
	}
	wg.Go(func() { _, cancelErr = e.svc.Cancel(t.Context(), c.ID, cancelOf(c)) })
	wg.Wait()

	got, _ := e.svc.Get(t.Context(), c.ID)
	plans := len(e.store.Plans(c.ID))
	switch got.Status {
	case campaign.StatusApproved: // release won
		if plans != 1 || cancelErr == nil {
			t.Errorf("released: plans = %d, cancel error = %v; want 1 plan and a failed cancel", plans, cancelErr)
		}
		requireCode(t, cancelErr, campaign.CodeInvalidState)
	case campaign.StatusDraftsReady: // cancel won
		if plans != 0 || cancelErr != nil {
			t.Errorf("canceled: plans = %d, cancel error = %v", plans, cancelErr)
		}
	default:
		t.Fatalf("status = %s", got.Status)
	}
}
EOF

EOF

//go:build integration

// Package pgtest starts a disposable PostgreSQL container for integration
// tests and applies the repository's migrations to it. Docker is required.
package pgtest

import (
	"errors"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/golang-migrate/migrate/v4"
	_ "github.com/golang-migrate/migrate/v4/database/pgx/v5" // registers the pgx5:// driver
	_ "github.com/golang-migrate/migrate/v4/source/file"     // registers the file:// source
	"github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go"
	tcpostgres "github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go/modules/postgres"
	"github.com/testcontainers/testcontainers-go/wait"
)

// Image matches the postgres service in docker-compose.yml.
const Image = "postgres:18.6-alpine"

// Start runs a PostgreSQL container that is terminated when the test ends
// and returns its connection string.
func Start(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	ctx := t.Context()

	ctr, err := tcpostgres.Run(ctx, Image,
		tcpostgres.WithDatabase("campaigns_test"),
		tcpostgres.WithUsername("test"),
		tcpostgres.WithPassword("test"),
		testcontainers.WithWaitStrategy(
			wait.ForLog("database system is ready to accept connections").
				WithOccurrence(2).
				WithStartupTimeout(60*time.Second),
		),
	)
	testcontainers.CleanupContainer(t, ctr)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("start postgres container: %v", err)
	}

	dsn, err := ctr.ConnectionString(ctx, "sslmode=disable")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("postgres connection string: %v", err)
	}
	return dsn
}

// Migrate applies every up migration in migrations/ to the database at dsn
// and returns the resulting schema version.
func Migrate(t *testing.T, dsn string) uint {
	t.Helper()
	m := newMigrate(t, dsn)
	if err := m.Up(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, migrate.ErrNoChange) {
		t.Fatalf("apply migrations: %v", err)
	}
	version, dirty, err := m.Version()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("read migration version: %v", err)
	}
	if dirty {
		t.Fatalf("migration version %d is dirty", version)
	}
	return version
}

// MigrateDown applies every down migration, leaving an empty schema.
func MigrateDown(t *testing.T, dsn string) {
	t.Helper()
	if err := newMigrate(t, dsn).Down(); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, migrate.ErrNoChange) {
		t.Fatalf("revert migrations: %v", err)
	}
}

func newMigrate(t *testing.T, dsn string) *migrate.Migrate {
	t.Helper()
	// golang-migrate selects its pgx v5 driver by the pgx5:// scheme.
	migrateURL, ok := strings.CutPrefix(dsn, "postgres://")
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected DSN scheme from testcontainers")
	}
	m, err := migrate.New("file://"+MigrationsDir(t), "pgx5://"+migrateURL)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("init migrate: %v", err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _, _ = m.Close() })
	return m
}

// MigrationsDir returns the absolute path of the repository's migrations/.
func MigrationsDir(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	_, file, _, ok := runtime.Caller(0)
	if !ok {
		t.Fatal("cannot resolve pgtest source location")
	}
	return filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(file), "..", "..", "..", "migrations")
}

var refNow = time.Date(2026, 10, 22, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)

const day = 24 * time.Hour

// consent states for fixture users. "" means no consent row at all.
const (
	granted = "granted"
	denied  = "denied"
)

type fixtureUser struct {
	city, locale string
	sms, email   string
	cartAgo      []time.Duration
	orderAgo     []time.Duration
}

// fixture is a small dataset with hand-computed expectations for the
// example rule (Istanbul, cart within 30 days, no purchase within 30):
//
//	#   city       consent sms/email  cart   order  example rule
//	1   İstanbul   granted/granted     2d     -      match
//	2   istanbul   granted/denied     29d     -      match (lower-case data)
//	3   ISTANBUL   -/-                 1d     -      match (no consent rows)
//	4   İstanbul   -/granted          10d     -      match (locale en)
//	5   İstanbul   granted/granted    10d     3d     cart then purchased
//	6   İstanbul   granted/granted     5d    45d     match (old order)
//	7   İstanbul   granted/granted    31d     -      cart outside window
//	8   Ankara     granted/granted     2d     -      other city
//	9   İstanbul   granted/granted    -1d     -      cart after reference time
//	10  İstanbul   granted/granted    30d     -      match (window boundary)
var fixture = []fixtureUser{
	{city: "İstanbul", locale: "tr", sms: granted, email: granted, cartAgo: []time.Duration{2 * day}},
	{city: "istanbul", locale: "tr", sms: granted, email: denied, cartAgo: []time.Duration{29 * day}},
	{city: "ISTANBUL", locale: "tr", cartAgo: []time.Duration{1 * day}},
	{city: "İstanbul", locale: "en", email: granted, cartAgo: []time.Duration{10 * day}},
	{city: "İstanbul", locale: "tr", sms: granted, email: granted, cartAgo: []time.Duration{10 * day}, orderAgo: []time.Duration{3 * day}},
	{city: "İstanbul", locale: "tr", sms: granted, email: granted, cartAgo: []time.Duration{5 * day}, orderAgo: []time.Duration{45 * day}},
	{city: "İstanbul", locale: "tr", sms: granted, email: granted, cartAgo: []time.Duration{31 * day}},

	}
}

**🔧 Bash** — Write migration 000005 up and down

~~~~
cat > migrations/000005_scheduling.up.sql <<'EOF'
-- Scheduled delivery: a campaign approved for a later time is 'scheduled'
-- (approval recorded, no deliveries yet) until a worker releases it
-- (→ 'approved', deliveries created), its release checks fail
-- ('release_failed'), or it is not released within the grace period
-- ('expired'). Each of these three carries the approval, like the
-- approved-or-later states, until a cancel returns it to drafts_ready.
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP CONSTRAINT campaigns_status_check;
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD CONSTRAINT campaigns_status_check
    CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'audience_ready', 'drafts_ready', 'scheduled', 'release_failed', 'expired',
                      'approved', 'sending', 'completed'));
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP CONSTRAINT campaigns_approval_check;
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD CONSTRAINT campaigns_approval_check
    CHECK ((status IN ('scheduled', 'release_failed', 'expired', 'approved', 'sending', 'completed'))
           = (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL));

-- scheduled_at is required while scheduled and kept afterwards for audit
-- (a released campaign keeps it; one approved to send now has none). A
-- cancel clears it together with the approval.
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD COLUMN scheduled_at timestamptz;
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD CONSTRAINT campaigns_scheduled_at_check
    CHECK ((status NOT IN ('scheduled', 'release_failed', 'expired') OR scheduled_at IS NOT NULL)
           AND (status NOT IN ('draft', 'audience_ready', 'drafts_ready') OR scheduled_at IS NULL));

-- Why a release failed: error code, message, and field errors. Never
-- message content or recipient data.
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD COLUMN release_failure jsonb;
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD CONSTRAINT campaigns_release_failure_check
    CHECK ((status = 'release_failed') = (release_failure IS NOT NULL)
           AND (release_failure IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(release_failure) = 'object'));

-- The release loop's due scan.
CREATE INDEX campaigns_scheduled_due_idx ON campaigns (scheduled_at) WHERE status = 'scheduled';

ALTER TABLE campaign_events DROP CONSTRAINT campaign_events_action_check;
ALTER TABLE campaign_events ADD CONSTRAINT campaign_events_action_check
    CHECK (action IN ('created', 'audience_proposed', 'drafts_generated', 'edited', 'approved',
                      'sending_started', 'completed',
                      'scheduled', 'rescheduled', 'schedule_canceled', 'released', 'release_failed', 'expired'));
EOF
cat > migrations/000005_scheduling.down.sql <<'EOF'
-- Campaigns approved for later but not sent cannot be represented by the
-- previous schema; they go back to drafts_ready without their approval,
-- as a cancel would. Released campaigns keep their status.
UPDATE campaigns SET status = 'drafts_ready', approved_by = NULL, approved_at = NULL, release_failure = NULL,
    scheduled_at = NULL, version = version + 1, updated_at = now()
WHERE status IN ('scheduled', 'release_failed', 'expired');

ALTER TABLE campaign_events DROP CONSTRAINT campaign_events_action_check;
DELETE FROM campaign_events
WHERE action IN ('scheduled', 'rescheduled', 'schedule_canceled', 'released', 'release_failed', 'expired');
ALTER TABLE campaign_events ADD CONSTRAINT campaign_events_action_check
    CHECK (action IN ('created', 'audience_proposed', 'drafts_generated', 'edited', 'approved',
                      'sending_started', 'completed'));

DROP INDEX IF EXISTS campaigns_scheduled_due_idx;
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP CONSTRAINT campaigns_release_failure_check;
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP COLUMN release_failure;
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP CONSTRAINT campaigns_scheduled_at_check;
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP COLUMN scheduled_at;

ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP CONSTRAINT campaigns_approval_check;
ALTER TABLE campaigns ADD CONSTRAINT campaigns_approval_check
    CHECK ((status IN ('approved', 'sending', 'completed')) = (approved_by IS NOT NULL AND approved_at IS NOT NULL));
ALTER TABLE campaigns DROP C

const testDatabaseURL = "postgres://app:s3cr3t-pw@localhost:5432/campaigns?sslmode=disable"

func envFrom(m map[string]string) Getenv {
	return func(key string) string { return m[key] }
}

func validEnv() map[string]string {
	return map[string]string{
		"DATABASE_URL":  testDatabaseURL,
		"KAFKA_BROKERS": "kafka:9092",
	}
}

func TestLoad_Defaults(t *testing.T) {
	cfg, err := Load(envFrom(validEnv()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Load() unexpected error: %v", err)
	}

	if cfg.LogLevel != slog.LevelInfo {
		t.Errorf("LogLevel = %v, want info", cfg.LogLevel)
	}
	if cfg.API.Addr != DefaultAPIAddr {
		t.Errorf("API.Addr = %q, want %q", cfg.API.Addr, DefaultAPIAddr)
	}
	if cfg.Worker.HealthAddr != DefaultWorkerHealthAddr {
		t.Errorf("Worker.HealthAddr = %q, want %q", cfg.Worker.HealthAddr, DefaultWorkerHealthAddr)
	}
	if cfg.ShutdownTimeout != DefaultShutdownTimeout {
		t.Errorf("ShutdownTimeout = %v, want %v", cfg.ShutdownTimeout, DefaultShutdownTimeout)
	}
	if cfg.ReadinessTimeout != DefaultReadinessTimeout {
		t.Errorf("ReadinessTimeout = %v, want %v", cfg.ReadinessTimeout, DefaultReadinessTimeout)
	}
	if cfg.API.RequestTimeout != DefaultRequestTimeout {
		t.Errorf("API.RequestTimeout = %v, want %v", cfg.API.RequestTimeout, DefaultRequestTimeout)
	}
	if cfg.DatabaseURL.Reveal() != testDatabaseURL {
		t.Errorf("DatabaseURL not preserved")
	}
	if len(cfg.KafkaBrokers) != 1 || cfg.KafkaBrokers[0] != "kafka:9092" {
		t.Errorf("KafkaBrokers = %v, want [kafka:9092]", cfg.KafkaBrokers)
	}
	if cfg.AnthropicAPIKey.IsSet() {
		t.Errorf("AnthropicAPIKey should be optional and empty by default")
	}
}

package delivery

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"
)

var discard = slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)

func TestDecodeJob(t *testing.T) {
	id, err := DecodeJob(EncodeJob("0199f0a0-0000-7000-8000-00000000000a"))
	if err != nil || id != "0199f0a0-0000-7000-8000-00000000000a" {
		t.Fatalf("round trip = %q, %v", id, err)
	}
	for _, bad := range []string{
		``, `{}`, `{"delivery_id":"x"}`, `{"delivery_id":"0199f0a0-0000-7000-8000-00000000000a","extra":1}`,
		`{"delivery_id":"0199f0a0-0000-7000-8000-00000000000a"}{}`, `{"delivery_id":"1'; DROP TABLE deliveries;--"}`,
	} {
		if _, err := DecodeJob([]byte(bad)); err == nil {
			t.Errorf("DecodeJob(%q) accepted", bad)
		}
	}
}

func TestHandle_DropsMalformedJobs(t *testing.T) {
	p := NewProcessor(nil, nil, nil, ProcessorConfig{}, discard) // nil ports: must not be reached
	if err := p.Handle(t.Context(), []byte(`not json`)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Handle() error = %v, want nil (drop)", err)
	}
}

// fakeOutbox holds pending messages; failNext makes the next publish fail.
type fakeOutbox struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	pending   []OutboxMessage
	published []OutboxMessage
	failNext  int
}

func (o *fakeOutbox) PublishPending(ctx context.Context, limit int, publish func(context.Context, []OutboxMessage) error) (int, error) {
	o.mu.Lock()
	defer o.mu.Unlock()
	batch := o.pending[:min(limit, len(o.pending))]
	if len(batch) == 0 {
		return 0, nil
	}
	if o.failNext > 0 {
		o.failNext--
		return 0, errors.New("broker unavailable")
	}
	if err := publish(ctx, batch); err != nil {
		return 0, err
	}
	o.published = append(o.published, batch...)
	o.pending = o.pending[len(batch):]
	return len(batch), nil
}

func (o *fakeOutbox) done() bool {
	o.mu.Lock()
	defer o.mu.Unlock()
	return len(o.pending) == 0
}

type nopPublisher struct{}

func (nopPublisher) Publish(context.Context, []OutboxMessage) error { return nil }

func TestRunRelay_PublishesEverythingAndSurvivesFailures(t *testing.T) {
	o := &fakeOutbox{failNext: 2}
	for i := range 25 {
		o.pending = append(o.pending, OutboxMessage{ID: int64(i + 1)})
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		done <- RunRelay(ctx, o, nopPublisher{}, RelayConfig{BatchSize: 10, PollInterval: time.Millisecond}, discard)
	}()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(2 * time.Second)
	for !o.done() && time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
	}
	cancel()
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("RunRelay() = %v", err)
	}
	if len(o.published) != 25 {
		t.Errorf("published %d messages, want 25", len(o.published))
	}
}

type fakeMaintenance struct {
	mu                         sync.Mutex
	due                        int
	recovered, requeued, ticks int
	completed, purged          int
}

func (m *fakeMaintenance) RecoverExpiredLeases(context.Context, int) (int, error) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	m.ticks++
	m.recovered++
	return 1, nil
}

func (m *fakeMaintenance) RequeueDue(_ context.Context, limit int) (int, error) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	n := min(limit, m.due)
	m.due -= n
	m.requeued += n
	return n, nil
}

func (m *fakeMaintenance) CompleteFinishedCampaigns(context.Context) (int, error) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	m.completed++
	return 0, nil
}

func (m *fakeMaintenance) PendingOutbox(context.Context) (int, error) { return 0, nil }

func (m *fakeMaintenance) PurgePublishedOutbox(context.Context, time.Duration) (int, error) {
	m.mu.Lock()
	defer m.mu.Unlock()
	m.purged++
	return 0, errors.New("ignored")
}

**🔧 Bash** — Add maintenance release test (expect red)

~~~~
cat >> internal/delivery/loops_test.go <<'EOF'

// fakeReleaser has due campaigns to release; failFirst fails its first
// call.
type fakeReleaser struct {
	mu        sync.Mutex
	due       int
	released  int
	failFirst bool
	calls     int
}

func (r *fakeReleaser) ReleaseDue(_ context.Context, limit int) (int, error) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.calls++
	if r.failFirst && r.calls == 1 {
		return 0, errors.New("database unavailable")
	}
	n := min(limit, r.due)
	r.due -= n
	r.released += n
	return n, nil
}

func (r *fakeReleaser) state() (released, calls int) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	return r.released, r.calls
}

func TestRunMaintenance_ReleasesDueCampaigns(t *testing.T) {
	m := &fakeMaintenance{}
	r := &fakeReleaser{due: 25, failFirst: true}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		done <- RunMaintenance(ctx, m, MaintenanceConfig{Interval: time.Millisecond, BatchSize: 10, Releaser: r}, discard)
	}()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(2 * time.Second)
	for {
		released, _ := r.state()
		if released == 25 || time.Now().After(deadline) {
			break
		}
		time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
	}
	cancel()
	if err := <-done; err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// The failed first call is retried on the next tick; full batches are
	// drained within a tick.
	if released, calls := r.state(); released != 25 || calls < 4 {
		t.Errorf("released = %d after %d calls, want 25 after at least 4", released, calls)
	}
}
EOF

cat > internal/platform/metrics/metrics_test.go <<'EOF'
package metrics

import (
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/prometheus/client_golang/prometheus"
	"github.com/prometheus/client_golang/prometheus/testutil"

	"github.com/oguzyildirim/smart-campaign-composer/internal/campaign"
)

func TestRelease(t *testing.T) {
	reg := prometheus.NewRegistry()
	m := NewRelease(reg)

	// Every outcome series exists from the start, so rates and alerts on
	// expired or failed releases are defined before the first one.
	want := `
# HELP campaign_releases_total Scheduled campaign releases by outcome (released, release_failed, expired).
# TYPE campaign_releases_total counter
campaign_releases_total{outcome="expired"} 0
campaign_releases_total{outcome="release_failed"} 0
campaign_releases_total{outcome="released"} 0
`
	if err := testutil.GatherAndCompare(reg, strings.NewReader(want), "campaign_releases_total"); err != nil {
		t.Error(err)
	}

	m.CampaignReleased(campaign.ReleaseReleased, 2*time.Second)
	m.CampaignReleased(campaign.ReleaseReleased, 4*time.Second)
	m.CampaignReleased(campaign.ReleaseExpired, 20*time.Minute)
	m.CampaignReleased(campaign.ReleaseOutcome("bogus"), time.Second) // never becomes a label

	want = `
# HELP campaign_releases_total Scheduled campaign releases by outcome (released, release_failed, expired).
# TYPE campaign_releases_total counter
campaign_releases_total{outcome="expired"} 1
campaign_releases_total{outcome="release_failed"} 0
campaign_releases_total{outcome="released"} 2
`
	if err := testutil.GatherAndCompare(reg, strings.NewReader(want), "campaign_releases_total"); err != nil {
		t.Error(err)
	}
	if n := testutil.CollectAndCount(m.lateness); n != 1 {
		t.Errorf("lateness series = %d, want 1 (no labels)", n)
	}
	h := &dtoHistogram{}
	h.read(t, reg, "campaign_release_lateness_seconds")
	if h.count != 3 || h.sum != 2+4+1200 {
		t.Errorf("lateness count = %d sum = %v, want 3 and 1206", h.count, h.sum)
	}
}

type dtoHistogram struct {
	count uint64
	sum   float64
}

func (h *dtoHistogram) read(t *testing.T, reg *prometheus.Registry, name string) {
	t.Helper()
	mfs, err := reg.Gather()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, mf := range mfs {
		if mf.GetName() == name {
			hist := mf.GetMetric()[0].GetHistogram()
			h.count, h.sum = hist.GetSampleCount(), hist.GetSampleSum()
			return
		}
	}
	t.Fatalf("metric %s not found", name)
}
EOF

// Release records scheduled campaign releases. It implements
// campaign.ReleaseMetrics.
type Release struct {
	releases *prometheus.CounterVec
	lateness prometheus.Histogram
}

var _ campaign.ReleaseMetrics = (*Release)(nil)

var releaseOutcomes = []campaign.ReleaseOutcome{campaign.ReleaseReleased, campaign.ReleaseFailed, campaign.ReleaseExpired}

// NewRelease registers the release metrics on reg.
func NewRelease(reg prometheus.Registerer) *Release {
	m := &Release{
		releases: prometheus.NewCounterVec(prometheus.CounterOpts{
			Name: "campaign_releases_total",
			Help: "Scheduled campaign releases by outcome (released, release_failed, expired).",
		}, []string{"outcome"}),
		lateness: prometheus.NewHistogram(prometheus.HistogramOpts{
			Name:    "campaign_release_lateness_seconds",
			Help:    "Time between a campaign's scheduled time and its release decision, for every outcome.",
			Buckets: []float64{.5, 1, 2.5, 5, 10, 30, 60, 300, 900, 3600},
		}),
	}
	reg.MustRegister(m.releases, m.lateness)
	for _, o := range releaseOutcomes {
		m.releases.WithLabelValues(string(o))
	}
	return m
}

// CampaignReleased implements campaign.ReleaseMetrics. Outcomes outside
// the known set are dropped, so labels stay bounded.
func (m *Release) CampaignReleased(o campaign.ReleaseOutcome, lateness time.Duration) {
	if !slices.Contains(releaseOutcomes, o) {
		return
	}
	m.releases.WithLabelValues(string(o)).Inc()
	m.lateness.Observe(lateness.Seconds())
}
EOF

    ports:

        condition: service_healthy
      kafka:
        condition: service_healthy
      migrate:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
      # Previews are only meaningful once the seed has committed.
      seed:
        condition: service_completed_successfully
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "/app", "healthcheck", "http://127.0.0.1:8080/readyz"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5
      start_period: 5s

  # Safe to scale: docker compose up -d --scale worker=3. Workers share
  # one consumer group, claim deliveries exclusively in Postgres, and share
  # each channel's rate limit through Postgres.
  worker:

      context: .

      RETRY_BASE_DELAY: ${RETRY_BASE_DELAY:-1s}
      RETRY_MAX_DELAY: ${RETRY_MAX_DELAY:-60s}
      RETRY_BACKOFF_FACTOR: ${RETRY_BACKOFF_FACTOR:-2}
      DELIVERY_LEASE: ${DELIVERY_LEASE:-60s}
      WORKER_CONCURRENCY: ${WORKER_CONCURRENCY:-16}
      RATE_LIMIT_PER_SECOND: ${RATE_LIMIT_PER_SECOND:-90}
      RATE_LIMIT_BURST: ${RATE_LIMIT_BURST:-5}
      KAFKA_TOPIC_PARTITIONS: ${KAFKA_TOPIC_PARTITIONS:-6}
    # /healthz, /readyz and /metrics on an ephemeral host port per replica
    # (scaling-safe); find it with: docker compose port --index 1 worker 8081
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1::8081"
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
      kafka:
        condition: service_healthy

Both services load the same config, so the `SCHEDULE_*` variables go into the shared `app-env` anchor.

**🔧 Bash** — Add schedule vars to compose and .env.example; validate compose
